    local function loadVabedoFont()
        local getAsset = getcustomasset or getsynasset
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" or type(getAsset) ~= "function" then return nil end
        local ttfName, jsonName = "RivalHub_Vabedo.ttf", "RivalHub_Vabedo.json"
        local okAll, result = pcall(function()
            local need = true
            if type(isfile) == "function" then
                local okF, has = pcall(isfile, ttfName)
                if okF and has then need = false end
            end
            if need then
                local chars = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
                local map = {}
                for i = 1, #chars do map[chars:sub(i, i)] = i - 1 end
                local out, n = {}, 0
                local buf, bits = 0, 0
                for i = 1, #VABEDO_B64 do
                    local v = map[VABEDO_B64:sub(i, i)]
                    if v then
                        buf = buf * 64 + v
                        bits = bits + 6
                        if bits >= 8 then
                            bits = bits - 8
                            local byte = math.floor(buf / (2 ^ bits))
                            buf = buf - byte * (2 ^ bits)
                            n = n + 1
                            out[n] = string.char(byte)
                        end
                    end
                end
                local raw = {}
                for i = 1, n, 4096 do raw[#raw + 1] = table.concat(out, "", i, math.min(i + 4095, n)) end
                writefile(ttfName, table.concat(raw))
            end
            local fam = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode({
                name = "Vabedo",
                faces = {{ name = "Regular", weight = 400, style = "normal", assetId = getAsset(ttfName) }}
            })
            writefile(jsonName, fam)
            return Font.new(getAsset(jsonName), Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
        end)
        if okAll then return result end
        return nil
    end

    local miniTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", miniBtn)
    miniTitle.Name = "RivalHubTitle"
    miniTitle.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.7, 0)
    miniTitle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    miniTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    miniTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    miniTitle.Text = "RIVAL HUB"
    miniTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    miniTitle.TextScaled = true
    miniTitle.TextWrapped = false
    miniTitle.TextSize = 44
    do
        local miniTitleLimit = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", miniTitle)
        miniTitleLimit.MaxTextSize = 38
        miniTitleLimit.MinTextSize = 8
    end
    miniTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 227, 40)
    miniTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    miniTitle.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    miniTitle.ZIndex = 23
    do
        local vabedo = loadVabedoFont()
        if vabedo then pcall(function() miniTitle.FontFace = vabedo end) end
    end

    local _mainAnimating = false
    local mainOriginalPos = main.Position
    local mainFullSize = main.Size
    _G.__RivalHubMainOriginalPos = mainOriginalPos
    main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
        if not _mainAnimating then
            mainOriginalPos = main.Position
            _G.__RivalHubMainOriginalPos = mainOriginalPos
        end
    end)

    local animScale = Instance.new("UIScale", main)
    animScale.Scale = 1
    local _openTween, _closeTween, _posTween = nil, nil, nil

    -- Animacion "cortinas": al cerrar se juntan desde AMBOS lados; al abrir se separan igual.
    -- El menu nunca cambia de tamano ni de lugar.
    local _curtains = nil
    local function destroyCurtains()
        if _curtains then
            for _, c in ipairs(_curtains) do pcall(function() c:Destroy() end) end
            _curtains = nil
        end
    end
    local function makeCurtains(frac)
        destroyCurtains()
        local bgc = main.BackgroundColor3
        -- color de la animacion: el elegido en GUI Color (o dorado por defecto)
        local animCol = (rivalGuiColored and selectedColor) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 227, 40)
        if rivalGuiColored and selectedColor then bgc = bgc:Lerp(animCol, 0.14) end
        local function mk(right)
            local f = Instance.new("Frame", main)
            f.Name = "AnimCurtain"
            f.BackgroundColor3 = bgc
            f.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            f.BorderSizePixel = 0
            f.Active = true
            f.ZIndex = 1000
            f.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(right and 1 or 0, 0)
            f.Position = UDim2.new(right and 1 or 0, 0, 0, 0)
            f.Size = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 1, 0)
            local edge = Instance.new("Frame", f)
            edge.BorderSizePixel = 0
            edge.BackgroundColor3 = animCol
            edge.ZIndex = 1001
            edge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0)
            edge.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(right and 0 or 1, 0)
            edge.Position = UDim2.new(right and 0 or 1, 0, 0, 0)
            return f
        end
        local l, r = mk(false), mk(true)
        _curtains = {l, r}
        return l, r
    end

    local function cancelAnims()
        if _openTween then _openTween:Cancel(); _openTween = nil end
        if _closeTween then _closeTween:Cancel(); _closeTween = nil end
        if _posTween then _posTween:Cancel(); _posTween = nil end
        destroyCurtains()
    end

    showGui = function()
        if not main then return end
        cancelAnims()
        _mainAnimating = true
        main.Size = mainFullSize
        main.Rotation = 0
        animScale.Scale = 1
        main.Position = mainOriginalPos
        local l, r = makeCurtains(0.5)
        main.Visible = true
        miniBtn.Visible = false
        local info = TweenInfo.new(0.38, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        _openTween = TS:Create(l, info, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
        TS:Create(r, info, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
        _openTween.Completed:Connect(function(state)
            if state ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
            _openTween = nil
            destroyCurtains()
            _mainAnimating = false
        end)
        _openTween:Play()
    end

    hideGui = function()
        if not main or not main.Visible then return end
        cancelAnims()
        _mainAnimating = true
        local l, r = makeCurtains(0)
        local info = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
        _closeTween = TS:Create(l, info, {Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)})
        TS:Create(r, info, {Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
        _closeTween.Completed:Connect(function(state)
            if state ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then return end
            _closeTween = nil
            main.Visible = false
            destroyCurtains()
            animScale.Scale = 1
            main.Size = mainFullSize
            main.Position = mainOriginalPos
            miniBtn.Visible = true
            _mainAnimating = false
        end)
        _closeTween:Play()
    end

    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(hideGui)
    miniBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(showGui)

    local tabBar = Instance.new("Frame", main)

    tabBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 134)

        local tabStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
        tabStroke.Name = "TabStroke"
        tabStroke.Color = getThemeColor()
        tabStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        tabStroke.Transparency = 1

        local underline = Instance.new("Frame", btn)
        underline.Name = "Underline"

        underline.ZIndex = 12
        Instance.new("UICorner", underline).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", tabContent)
        page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        page.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        page.BorderSizePixel = 0
        page.ClipsDescendants = true
        page.ScrollBarThickness = 2
        page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = getThemeColor()
        page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.3
        page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        page.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        page.ZIndex = 6
        page.Visible = (i == 1)

        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", page)
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center

        local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding", page)
        padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
        padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 20)

        contentPages[name] = page

        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            for _, pg2 in pairs(contentPages) do pg2.Visible = false end
            page.Visible = true
            for _, b in ipairs(tabButtons) do
                local ul = b:FindFirstChild("Underline")
                if ul then ul.Visible = false end
                local bs = b:FindFirstChild("TabStroke")
                TS:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {
                    BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                    BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 34),
                    TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 165)
                }):Play()
                if bs then TS:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {Transparency = 1}):Play() end
            end
            local ul = btn:FindFirstChild("Underline")
            if ul then ul.Visible = true end
            local bs = btn:FindFirstChild("TabStroke")
            TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
                BackgroundColor3 = getThemeColor(),
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
            }):Play()
            if bs then TS:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {Transparency = 0.3}):Play() end
        end)

        btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if btn:FindFirstChild("Underline") and btn.Underline.Visible then return end
            TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.55,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 215, 230)
            }):Play()
        end)
        btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if btn:FindFirstChild("Underline") and btn.Underline.Visible then return end
            TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 165)
            }):Play()
        end)

        table.insert(tabButtons, btn)
    end

    if tabButtons[1] then
        local ul = tabButtons[1]:FindFirstChild("Underline")
        if ul then ul.Visible = true end
        tabButtons[1].BackgroundColor3 = getThemeColor()
        tabButtons[1].BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        tabButtons[1].TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
        local bs = tabButtons[1]:FindFirstChild("TabStroke")
        if bs then bs.Transparency = 0.3 end
    end

    local pageCounters = {}

    local function getNextOrder(page)
        if not pageCounters[page] then pageCounters[page] = 0 end
        pageCounters[page] = pageCounters[page] + 1
        return pageCounters[page]
    end

    local function mkSect(page, txt)

    end

    local function mkRow(page, h)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame", page)
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, h or 42)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BG
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
        f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.LayoutOrder = getNextOrder(page)
        f.ZIndex = 7
        Instance.new("UICorner", f).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11)

        local rowGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", f)
        rowGrad.Name = "RowGrad"
        rowGrad.Rotation = 90
        rowGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, 0.00),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, 0.30),
        })

        local rowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", f)
        rowStroke.Color = ROW_BORDER
        rowStroke.Thickness = 1
        rowStroke.Transparency = 0.55

        f.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22,22,26)}):Play()
            TS:Create(rowStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Color = getThemeColor(), Transparency = 0.35}):Play()
        end)
        f.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(f, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {BackgroundColor3 = ROW_BG}):Play()
            TS:Create(rowStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {Color = ROW_BORDER, Transparency = 0.55}):Play()
        end)
        return f
    end

    end

    local function mkPill(row, offset)
        local pill = Instance.new("Frame", row)
        pill.Name = "Track"
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 18)
        pill.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)

        pill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 47, 58)
        pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pill.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
        local trackStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pill)
        trackStroke.Name = "TrackStroke"
        trackStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(88, 90, 106)
        trackStroke.Thickness = 1
        trackStroke.Transparency = 0.2

        local dot = Instance.new("Frame", pill)
        dot.Name = "Knob"
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 207, 218)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
        local dotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", dot)
        dotStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(150, 152, 168)
        dotStroke.Thickness = 1
        dotStroke.Transparency = 0.15
        dotStroke.Name = "KnobStroke"
        return pill, dot
    end

    local function animPill(pill, dot, on)
        local dotStroke = dot:FindFirstChild("KnobStroke")
        local trackStroke = pill:FindFirstChild("TrackStroke")
        local fadeInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        local c = getThemeColor()
        TS:Create(pill, fadeInfo, {BackgroundColor3 = on and c or Color3.fromRGB(45,47,58)}):Play()
        TS:Create(dot, fadeInfo, {
            Position = on and UDim2.new(1, -9, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or Color3.fromRGB(205,207,218),
        }):Play()
        if trackStroke then TS:Create(trackStroke, fadeInfo, {Color = on and c or Color3.fromRGB(88,90,106)}):Play() end
        if dotStroke then TS:Create(dotStroke, fadeInfo, {Color = on and c or Color3.fromRGB(150,152,168)}):Play() end
    end

    local function mkToggle(page, txt, cb)
        local row = mkRow(page, 42)
        mkLabel(row, txt)
        local pill, dot = mkPill(row, 62)
        local on = false
        local function sv(s) on = s; animPill(pill, dot, s) end
        local clk = Instance.new("TextButton", pill)
        clk.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        clk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clk.Text = ""
        clk.AutoButtonColor = false
        clk.ZIndex = 10
        clk.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            on = not on
            sv(on)
            pcall(cb, on)
            task.defer(function() pcall(saveAllSettings) end)
        end)
        return sv, row
    end

    local function mkSelector(parent, default, options, cb)
        local container = Instance.new("Frame", parent)
        container.Size = UDim2.new(0, 160, 1, 0)
        container.Position = UDim2.new(1, -168, 0, 0)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.ZIndex = 8
        local leftBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", container)
        leftBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
        leftBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -13)
        leftBtn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        leftBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
        leftBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        leftBtn.Text = "<"
        leftBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
        leftBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        leftBtn.TextSize = 13
        leftBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        leftBtn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", leftBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", container)
        label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 26)
        label.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -40, 0.5, -13)
        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        label.Text = default
        label.TextColor3 = WHITE
        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        label.TextSize = 12
        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        label.ZIndex = 9
        local rightBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", container)
        rightBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
        rightBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, -13)
        rightBtn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        rightBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
        rightBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        rightBtn.Text = ">"
        rightBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
        rightBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        rightBtn.TextSize = 13
        rightBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
        rightBtn.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", rightBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local function updateLabel(newText) label.Text = newText end
        leftBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if cb then cb(-1, updateLabel); task.defer(function() pcall(saveAllSettings) end) end
        end)
        rightBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if cb then cb(1, updateLabel); task.defer(function() pcall(saveAllSettings) end) end
        end)
        return label
    end

    local function mkToggleSelector(page, txt, cb, options, getKey, onSelect)
        local sv, row = mkToggle(page, txt, cb)
        local n = #options

        local arrow = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        arrow.Name = "SelectorArrow"
        arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 26)
        arrow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, 0)
        arrow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        arrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        arrow.Text = "▼"
        arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        arrow.TextSize = 11
        arrow.AutoButtonColor = false
        arrow.ZIndex = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", arrow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        local arrowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", arrow)
        arrowStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        arrowStroke.Transparency = 0.25

        local dd = Instance.new("Frame", page)
        dd.Name = "SelectorOptions"
        dd.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0)
        dd.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        dd.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        dd.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dd.LayoutOrder = getNextOrder(page)
        dd.Visible = false
        dd.ClipsDescendants = true
        dd.ZIndex = 7
        Instance.new("UICorner", dd).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11)
        local ddStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", dd)
        ddStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        ddStroke.Transparency = 0.35

        local slider = Instance.new("Frame", dd)
        slider.Size = UDim2.new(1 / n, -6, 1, -8)
        slider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
        slider.BorderSizePixel = 0
        slider.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", slider).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)
        local sliderGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", slider)
        sliderGrad.Rotation = 90
        sliderGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0))
        local sliderStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", slider)
        sliderStroke.Thickness = 1
        sliderStroke.Transparency = 0.2

        local labels = {}
        local function selIndex()
            local cur = getKey()
            for i, o in ipairs(options) do
                if o[1] == cur then return i end
            end
            return 1
        end

        local function paint(animate)
            local selTxt = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 60)
            local offTxt = Color3.fromRGB(201, 148, 0)
            local arrowCol = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
            if rivalGuiColored and selectedColor then
                selTxt = selectedColor
                offTxt = selectedColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 0.45)
                arrowCol = selectedColor
            end
            local idx = selIndex()
            local pos = UDim2.new((idx - 1) / n, 3, 0, 4)
            slider.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            sliderStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            sliderStroke.Transparency = 1
            ddStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            ddStroke.Transparency = 1
            arrowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            arrow.TextColor3 = arrowCol
            if animate then
                TS:Create(slider, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), { Position = pos }):Play()
            else
                slider.Position = pos
            end
            for i, l in ipairs(labels) do
                if i == idx then
                    l.TextColor3 = selTxt
                    l.TextTransparency = 0
                    l.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
                else
                    l.TextColor3 = offTxt
                    l.TextTransparency = 0
                    l.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
                end
            end
        end

        for i, o in ipairs(options) do
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", dd)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1 / n, 0, 1, 0)
            lbl.Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) / n, 0, 0, 0)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Text = string.upper(tostring(o[2]))
            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            lbl.TextSize = 12
            lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            lbl.ZIndex = 9
            labels[i] = lbl
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton", dd)
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1 / n, 0, 1, 0)
            b.Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) / n, 0, 0, 0)
            b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            b.Text = ""
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.ZIndex = 11
            b.Activated:Connect(function()
                pcall(onSelect, o[1])
                paint(true)
                task.defer(function() pcall(saveAllSettings) end)
            end)
        end
        paint(false)
        _rgSelRepaint[#_rgSelRepaint + 1] = function() paint(false) end

        local open, tw = false, nil
        arrow.Activated:Connect(function()
            open = not open
            if tw then tw:Cancel() end
            if open then
                paint(false)
                dd.Visible = true
                arrow.Text = "▲"
                arrowStroke.Transparency = 0
                tw = TS:Create(dd, TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 40) })
                tw:Play()
            else
                arrow.Text = "▼"
                arrowStroke.Transparency = 0.25
                tw = TS:Create(dd, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0) })
                tw.Completed:Connect(function() if not open then dd.Visible = false end end)
                tw:Play()
            end
        end)

        return sv, function() paint(false) end
    end

    local function mkBox(parent, default, w, xOff, cb)
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox", parent)
        local bw = w or 50
        local xo = math.max(xOff or 56, bw + 12)
        tb.Size = UDim2.new(0, bw, 0, 24)
        tb.Position = UDim2.new(1, -xo, 0.5, -12)
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        tb.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
        tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Text = tostring(default)
        tb.TextColor3 = WHITE
        tb.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        tb.TextSize = 11
        tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        tb.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", tb).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", tb)
        bs.Color = ROW_BORDER
        bs.Thickness = 1.2
        bs.Transparency = 0.25
        tb.Focused:Connect(function() TS:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {Color = getThemeColor(), Transparency = 0}):Play() end)
        tb.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            TS:Create(bs, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {Color = ROW_BORDER, Transparency = 0.25}):Play()
            if cb then
                local n = tonumber(tb.Text)
                if n then cb(n) else tb.Text = tostring(default) end
                task.defer(function() saveAllSettings() end)
            end
        end)
        return tb
    end

    local function mkKeyButton(parent, kbEntry)
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", parent)
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 80, 0, 60)
        btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -88, 0.5, -30)
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = INP
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local function getLabel() return (kbEntry.gp and kbEntry.gp.Name) or (kbEntry.kb and kbEntry.kb.Name) or "None" end
        btn.Text = getLabel()
        btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
        btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        btn.TextSize = 11
        btn.ZIndex = 8
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
        bs.Color = ROW_BORDER
        bs.Thickness = 1
        local li = false; local lc; local pv = btn.Text; local listenStart = 0
        btn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if li then li = false; _anyKeyListening = false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc = nil end; btn.Text = pv; btn.TextColor3 = WHITE; return end
            pv = btn.Text; li = true; _anyKeyListening = true; listenStart = _tick(); btn.Text = "..."; btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
            lc = UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if not li then return end
                if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then li = false; _anyKeyListening = false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc = nil end; btn.Text = pv; btn.TextColor3 = WHITE; return end
                local isGp = isGamepadInput(inp)
                if isGp and _tick()-listenStart < 0.15 then return end
                if not isBindableInput(inp) then return end
                btn.Text = inp.KeyCode.Name; pv = inp.KeyCode.Name; btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
                li = false; _anyKeyListening = false; if lc then lc:Disconnect(); lc = nil end
                if isGp then kbEntry.gp = inp.KeyCode; kbEntry.kb = nil else kbEntry.kb = inp.KeyCode; kbEntry.gp = nil end
                task.defer(function() saveAllSettings() end)
            end)
        end)
        table.insert(keyButtonRefs, {btn = btn, entry = kbEntry})
        return btn
    end

    local function addKeybindRow(page, labelText, kbEntry)

    end

-------------------------------------------------
-- TEMAS DE COLOR (cambian toda la GUI)
-------------------------------------------------
local ACCENT_KEYS = {"greenNeon", "greenHot", "greenBright", "greenSoft", "greenGlow", "greenDeep", "greenDarker", "greenBurn"}

local ORIGINAL_ACCENT = {}
for _, k in ipairs(ACCENT_KEYS) do ORIGINAL_ACCENT[k] = COLORS[k] end

-- {saturación, brillo} de cada tono, calculados desde el verde original
local HSV_PROFILE = {
    greenNeon   = {1,     1},
    greenHot    = {1,     0.90},
    greenBright = {0.843, 1},
    greenSoft   = {0.686, 1},
    greenGlow   = {0.529, 1},
    greenDeep   = {0.917, 0.235},
    greenDarker = {1,     0.157},
    greenBurn   = {1,     0.098},
}

local THEMES = {
    {name = "Morado",   hue = 262/360, swatch = Color3.fromRGB(145, 105, 250)},
    {name = "Azul",     hue = 218/360, swatch = Color3.fromRGB(75, 130, 240)},
    {name = "Rojo",     hue = 0,       swatch = Color3.fromRGB(215, 60, 60)},
    {name = "Rosa",     hue = 325/360, swatch = Color3.fromRGB(240, 115, 180)},
    {name = "Amarillo", hue = 48/360,  swatch = Color3.fromRGB(250, 215, 70)},
    {name = "Oscuro",   gray = {greenNeon = 0.35, greenHot = 0.28, greenBright = 0.45, greenSoft = 0.52, greenGlow = 0.62, greenDeep = 0.10, greenDarker = 0.06, greenBurn = 0.03},
                        swatch = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 45), tint = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 115)},
    {name = "Blanco",   gray = {greenNeon = 0.58, greenHot = 0.50, greenBright = 0.80, greenSoft = 0.88, greenGlow = 0.95, greenDeep = 0.26, greenDarker = 0.18, greenBurn = 0.10},
                        swatch = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 245), tint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)},
    {name = "Verde",    original = true, swatch = Color3.fromRGB(170, 255, 0), tint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)},
}

local function buildPalette(theme)
    if theme.original then return ORIGINAL_ACCENT end
    local pal = {}
    for _, k in ipairs(ACCENT_KEYS) do
        if theme.gray then
            pal[k] = Color3.fromHSV(0, 0, theme.gray[k])
        else
            local sv = HSV_PROFILE[k]
            pal[k] = Color3.fromHSV(theme.hue, sv[1], sv[2])
        end
    end
    return pal
end

local function themeTint(theme)
    if theme.tint then return theme.tint end
    return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255):Lerp(Color3.fromHSV(theme.hue, 1, 1), 0.55)
end

-- Cambia COLORS y devuelve la tabla {viejo, nuevo} para recolorear lo ya creado
local function applyPalette(idx)
    local pal = buildPalette(THEMES[idx])
    local map = {}
    for _, k in ipairs(ACCENT_KEYS) do
        table.insert(map, {COLORS[k], pal[k]})
        COLORS[k] = pal[k]
    end
    return map
end

local resolvedCache = {}
local readyUrl = {}
local BG_CACHE_FILE = "RivalBgCache_v2.json"

pcall(function()
    if isfile and readfile and isfile(BG_CACHE_FILE) then
        local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(BG_CACHE_FILE))
        if type(data) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(data) do
                if type(k) == "string" and type(v) == "string" then readyUrl[k] = v end
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function saveBgCache()
    if not writefile then return end
    pcall(function() writefile(BG_CACHE_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(readyUrl)) end)
end

local function resolveDecalTexture(id)
    if resolvedCache[id] ~= nil then return resolvedCache[id] or nil end
    local result = false
    pcall(function()
        local objs = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. id)
        for _, obj in ipairs(objs) do
            local found = obj
            if not (found:IsA("Decal") or found:IsA("Texture")) then
                found = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal", true) or obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Texture", true)
            end
            if found and found.Texture and found.Texture ~= "" then
                result = found.Texture
                break
            end
        end
    end)
    resolvedCache[id] = result
    return result or nil
end

local function fetchOk(candidate, maxWait)
    local ok, done = false, false
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({candidate}, function(_, status)
                if status == Enum.AssetFetchStatus.Success then ok = true end
            end)
        end)
        done = true
    end)
    local w = 0
    while not done and w < maxWait do
        task.wait(0.05)
        w += 0.05
    end
    return ok
end

local inProgress = {}
local function getGoodUrl(url)
    local id = url and tostring(url):match("%d+")
    if not id then return nil end
    if readyUrl[id] then return readyUrl[id] end
    if inProgress[id] then
        local w = 0
        while inProgress[id] and w < 12 do task.wait(0.1); w += 0.1 end
        return readyUrl[id]
    end
    inProgress[id] = true
    local good
    local tex = resolveDecalTexture(id)
    if tex and fetchOk(tex, 3) then
        good = tex
    elseif fetchOk(url, 2) and not tex then
        good = url
    end
    if not good then
        local thumb = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. id .. "&w=420&h=420"
        if fetchOk(thumb, 4) then good = thumb else good = thumb end
    end
    readyUrl[id] = good
    inProgress[id] = nil
    return good
end

local function loadImageInto(label, assetUrl, onDone)
    local id = tostring(assetUrl):match("%d+")
    local token = (label:GetAttribute("LoadToken") or 0) + 1
    label:SetAttribute("LoadToken", token)

    if id and readyUrl[id] then
        label.Image = ""
        label.Image = readyUrl[id]
        if onDone then onDone() end
        return
    end

    label.Image = ""
    task.spawn(function()
        local good = getGoodUrl(assetUrl)
        if label:GetAttribute("LoadToken") ~= token then return end
        label.Image = good or assetUrl
        if onDone then onDone() end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local pending = 0
    for i = 1, #BackgroundImages do
        pending += 1
        task.spawn(function()
            getGoodUrl(BackgroundImages[i])
            pending -= 1
        end)
    end
    local t = 0
    while pending > 0 and t < 15 do
        task.wait(0.2)
        t += 0.2
    end
    saveBgCache()
end)

-------------------------------------------------
-- CONFIG & SAVE
-------------------------------------------------
local CONFIG_FILE = "RivalConfuser_Config.json"

local DEFAULT_CFG = {
    guiPosXScale  = 0.5,
    guiPosXOffset = 0,
    guiPosYScale  = 0.2,
    guiPosYOffset = 0,
    guiScale      = 1,
    windowOpen    = true,
    bgPick        = 6,
    themePick     = 8,

    -- RIVAL CONFUSER
    rivalManual   = false,
    rivalAutoSteal = false,
    rivalMode     = "V1",
    rivalBind     = "NONE",
    rivalMinimized = false,
}

local cfg = {}
for k, v in pairs(DEFAULT_CFG) do cfg[k] = v end

local function resolveKey(name)
    if not name or name == "" or name == "NONE" or name == "None" then return nil end
    local ok, val = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[name] end)
    return (ok and val) or nil
end

local function saveConfig()
    local ok, encoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(cfg) end)
    if ok and encoded and writefile then
        pcall(writefile, CONFIG_FILE, encoded)
    end
end

local function loadConfig()
    if not (isfile and readfile and isfile(CONFIG_FILE)) then return end
    local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG_FILE)) end)
    if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return end

    for k, v in pairs(DEFAULT_CFG) do
        if type(data[k]) == type(v) then cfg[k] = data[k] end
    end
    cfg.bgPick = math.floor(tonumber(cfg.bgPick) or DEFAULT_CFG.bgPick)
    if cfg.bgPick < 1 or not BackgroundImages[cfg.bgPick] then cfg.bgPick = DEFAULT_CFG.bgPick end
    cfg.guiScale = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.guiScale) or 1, 0.5, 2.0)
    if not ({V1=true, V2=true, V3=true, VALL=true})[cfg.rivalMode] then cfg.rivalMode = "V1" end
    cfg.themePick = math.floor(tonumber(cfg.themePick) or DEFAULT_CFG.themePick)
    if cfg.themePick < 1 or cfg.themePick > #THEMES then cfg.themePick = DEFAULT_CFG.themePick end
end

loadConfig()
applyPalette(cfg.themePick)

local guiScale = cfg.guiScale
local LP = plr

-------------------------------------------------
-- ANTI DIE PRINCIPAL
-------------------------------------------------
local AntiDie = {
	enabled = false,
	loop = nil,
	healthConn = nil,
	charConn = nil,
	lastHealTime = 0,
	invincibleUntil = 0,
	config = {
		healthThreshold = 50,
		invincibilityFrames = 0.75,
		ragdollProtection = true,
		autoRevive = true,
	},
}

function AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum)
	if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
	local maxHealth = hum.MaxHealth or 100
	if maxHealth <= 0 or maxHealth == math.huge then maxHealth = 100 end
	pcall(function()
		hum.Health = maxHealth
		if hum.MaxHealth < maxHealth then hum.MaxHealth = maxHealth end
	end)
	AntiDie.invincibleUntil = tick() + AntiDie.config.invincibilityFrames
	AntiDie.lastHealTime = tick()
	pcall(function()
		local char = hum.Parent
		if not char then return end
		for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("NumberValue") then
				local name = child.Name:lower()
				if name:find("health") or name:find("hp") or name:find("life") then
					child.Value = maxHealth
				end
			end
			if child:IsA("BoolValue") and child.Name:lower():find("dead") then
				child.Value = false
			end
		end
	end)
end

function AntiDie.PreventDamage(root, hum)
	if not hum then return end
	if hum.Health < (hum.MaxHealth or 100) then AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum) end
	if tick() < AntiDie.invincibleUntil then
		if hum.Health < (hum.MaxHealth or 100) then
			hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth or 100
		end
	end
	if AntiDie.config.ragdollProtection then
		local st = hum:GetState()
		if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or
		   st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or
		   st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or
		   st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
			pcall(function()
				hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
				hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
			end)
			AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum)
			if root then
				pcall(function() root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
			end
		end
	end
	if hum.Health <= 0 then
		AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum)
		pcall(function()
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
		end)
		if root then
			pcall(function()
				root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
	end
end

function AntiDie.AutoRevive()
	if not AntiDie.config.autoRevive then return end
	local char = LP.Character
	if not char then return end
	local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not hum then return end
	if hum.Health <= 0 then
		AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum)
		pcall(function()
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
		end)
		if root then
			pcall(function()
				root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
				root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
			end)
		end
	end
end

function AntiDie.AttachHealth(char)
	if AntiDie.healthConn then AntiDie.healthConn:Disconnect(); AntiDie.healthConn = nil end
	local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not hum then hum = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3) end
	if not hum then return end
	AntiDie.healthConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
		if not AntiDie.enabled then return end
		if hum.Health < (hum.MaxHealth or 100) then AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum) end
		if hum.Health <= 0 then AntiDie.AutoRevive() end
	end)
	if hum.Health < (hum.MaxHealth or 100) then AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum) end
end

function AntiDie.Start()
	if AntiDie.enabled and AntiDie.loop then return end
	AntiDie.enabled = true
	if AntiDie.loop then AntiDie.loop:Disconnect(); AntiDie.loop = nil end
	if AntiDie.healthConn then AntiDie.healthConn:Disconnect(); AntiDie.healthConn = nil end

	AntiDie.loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if not AntiDie.enabled then return end
		local char = LP.Character
		if not char then return end
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hum then return end
		if hum.Health <= 0 then
			AntiDie.AutoRevive()
		elseif hum.Health <= AntiDie.config.healthThreshold then
			AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum)
		elseif hum.Health < (hum.MaxHealth or 100) then
			AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum)
		end
		AntiDie.PreventDamage(root, hum)
	end)

	if LP.Character then AntiDie.AttachHealth(LP.Character) end
	if AntiDie.charConn then AntiDie.charConn:Disconnect(); AntiDie.charConn = nil end
	AntiDie.charConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
		if not AntiDie.enabled then return end
		task.wait(0.05)
		AntiDie.AttachHealth(char)
		local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then AntiDie.SuperHeal(hum) end
	end)
end

function AntiDie.Stop()
	AntiDie.enabled = false
	if AntiDie.loop then AntiDie.loop:Disconnect(); AntiDie.loop = nil end
	if AntiDie.healthConn then AntiDie.healthConn:Disconnect(); AntiDie.healthConn = nil end
	if AntiDie.charConn then AntiDie.charConn:Disconnect(); AntiDie.charConn = nil end
	pcall(function()
		local char = LP.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not hum then return end
		hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
		hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
		hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
		hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = true
		if hum.MaxHealth == math.huge or hum.MaxHealth <= 0 then
			hum.MaxHealth = 100
			hum.Health = math.min(hum.Health, 100)
		end
	end)
end

-------------------------------------------------
-- RIVAL CONFUSER RUNTIME
-------------------------------------------------
local Rival = {
    alive = true,
    connections = {},
    tracks = {},
    ids = {},
    enabled = false,
    forced = false,
    manual = cfg.rivalManual,
    mode = cfg.rivalMode,
    phase = 0,
    playConn = nil,
    savedAutoRotate = nil,
    antiDieChar = nil,
    antiDieHum = nil,
    antiDieConns = {},
    antiDieFF = nil,
    antiDieOrig = nil,
    antiDieLastHeal = 0,
}

-- Anti Die secundario del Rival
local function rivalUnbindAntiDie()
    for _, c in ipairs(Rival.antiDieConns) do
        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
    end
    table.clear(Rival.antiDieConns)
    local h, old = Rival.antiDieHum, Rival.antiDieOrig
    if h and h.Parent and old then
        pcall(function() h.BreakJointsOnDeath = old.breakJoints end)
        pcall(function() h.RequiresNeck = old.requiresNeck end)
        pcall(function() h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, old.deadEnabled) end)
    end
    if Rival.antiDieFF and Rival.antiDieFF.Parent then
        pcall(function() Rival.antiDieFF:Destroy() end)
    end
    Rival.antiDieChar, Rival.antiDieHum, Rival.antiDieOrig, Rival.antiDieFF = nil, nil, nil, nil
end

local function rivalApplyAntiDie(character)
    rivalUnbindAntiDie()
    if not Rival.alive or not character then return end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 8)
    if not humanoid or not Rival.alive or LP.Character ~= character then return end
    Rival.antiDieChar, Rival.antiDieHum = character, humanoid
    local original = {breakJoints = humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath,
        requiresNeck = humanoid.RequiresNeck, deadEnabled = true}
    pcall(function() original.deadEnabled = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) end)
    Rival.antiDieOrig = original
    local function protect()
        if not Rival.alive or Rival.antiDieHum ~= humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then return end
        pcall(function()
            humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            humanoid.RequiresNeck = false
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
        end)
    end
    local function heal()
        if not Rival.alive or Rival.antiDieHum ~= humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then return end
        if humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth and now - Rival.antiDieLastHeal >= 0.12 then
            Rival.antiDieLastHeal = now
            pcall(function() humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth end)
        end
    end
    protect()
    local ff = Instance.new("ForceField")
    ff.Name = "RivalAntiDie"
    ff.Visible = false
    ff.Parent = character
    Rival.antiDieFF = ff
    table.insert(Rival.antiDieConns, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(heal))
    table.insert(Rival.antiDieConns, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BreakJointsOnDeath"):Connect(protect))
    table.insert(Rival.antiDieConns, humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("RequiresNeck"):Connect(protect))
    heal()
end

table.insert(Rival.connections, LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
    task.defer(function() rivalApplyAntiDie(character) end)
end))
if LP.Character then task.defer(function() rivalApplyAntiDie(LP.Character) end) end

task.spawn(function()
    while Rival.alive do
        task.wait(1)
        local h = Rival.antiDieHum
        if h and h.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                h.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                h.RequiresNeck = false
                h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
            end)
            if not (Rival.antiDieFF and Rival.antiDieFF.Parent) and Rival.antiDieChar and Rival.antiDieChar.Parent then
                local ff = Instance.new("ForceField")
                ff.Name = "RivalAntiDie"
                ff.Visible = false
                ff.Parent = Rival.antiDieChar
                Rival.antiDieFF = ff
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- Emotes / animaciones
local EMOTES = {V1 = 125227030835962, V2 = 130836074181150, V3 = 129336210131513}

local function rivalStopTracks()
    for k, t in pairs(Rival.tracks) do
        pcall(function() t:Stop(0.12); t:Destroy() end)
        Rival.tracks[k] = nil
    end
end

local function rivalResolve(id)
    if Rival.ids[id] then return Rival.ids[id] end
    local found
    pcall(function()
        for _, o in ipairs(game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. id)) do
            for _, d in ipairs(o:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Animation") and d.AnimationId ~= "" then found = d.AnimationId; break end
            end
            if o:IsA("Animation") and o.AnimationId ~= "" then found = o.AnimationId end
            o:Destroy()
            if found then break end
        end
    end)
    if not found then pcall(function()
        local model = InsertService:LoadAsset(id)
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("Animation") and d.AnimationId ~= "" then found = d.AnimationId; break end
        end
        model:Destroy()
    end) end
    found = found or ("rbxassetid://" .. id)
    Rival.ids[id] = found
    return found
end

local function rivalEnsureTracks(hum)
    local wanted = {}
    if Rival.mode == "VALL" then
        wanted.V1 = true; wanted.V2 = true; wanted.V3 = true
    else
        wanted[Rival.mode] = true
    end
    for key, tr in pairs(Rival.tracks) do
        if not wanted[key] then
            pcall(function() tr:Stop(0.1); tr:Destroy() end)
            Rival.tracks[key] = nil
        end
    end
    for key in pairs(wanted) do
        local tr = Rival.tracks[key]
        if not tr or not tr.IsPlaying then
            pcall(function()
                local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if not animator then
                    animator = Instance.new("Animator")
                    animator.Parent = hum
                end
                local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                anim.AnimationId = rivalResolve(EMOTES[key])
                local track = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
                anim:Destroy()
                track.Looped = true
                track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
                track:Play(0.15)
                Rival.tracks[key] = track
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function rivalStop()
    Rival.enabled = false
    if Rival.playConn then Rival.playConn:Disconnect(); Rival.playConn = nil end
    rivalStopTracks()
    local c = LP.Character
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if h and Rival.savedAutoRotate ~= nil then h.AutoRotate = Rival.savedAutoRotate end
    Rival.savedAutoRotate = nil
end

local function rivalStart()
    rivalStop()
    Rival.enabled = true
    Rival.phase = 0
    local nextFlip = 0
    local nextTarget = 0
    local target = nil
    local nextEmotes = 0
    Rival.playConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not Rival.alive or not Rival.enabled then return end
        local c = LP.Character
        local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        if Rival.savedAutoRotate == nil then Rival.savedAutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate end
        hum.AutoRotate = false
        local now = os.clock()
        if now >= nextEmotes then rivalEnsureTracks(hum); nextEmotes = now + 0.35 end
        if now >= nextTarget then
            target = nil
            local best = math.huge
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= LP and p.Character then
                    local r = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if r and h and h.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (r.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < best then best = dist; target = r end
                    end
                end
            end
            nextTarget = now + 0.15
        end
        if now >= nextFlip then Rival.phase = (Rival.phase + 1) % 4; nextFlip = now + 0.008 end
        local delta = target and target.Parent and (target.Position - root.Position) or root.CFrame.LookVector
        local flat = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
        if flat.Magnitude < 0.05 then flat = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
        flat = flat.Unit
        local right = Vector3.new(-flat.Z, 0, flat.X)
        local phase = Rival.phase
        local look = (phase == 0 and flat) or (phase == 1 and right) or (phase == 2 and -flat) or -right
        pcall(function()
            root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, root.Position + look)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)
    end)
end

local function rivalApplyEnabled()
    local should = (cfg.rivalAutoSteal and Rival.forced) or (not cfg.rivalAutoSteal and Rival.manual)
    if should ~= Rival.enabled then
        if should then rivalStart() else rivalStop() end
    end
end

-- Detector de baja velocidad (auto-steal)
local lowSamples = 0
local highSamples = 0
local function rivalCheckSpeed()
    if not cfg.rivalAutoSteal then
        lowSamples = 0; highSamples = 0; Rival.forced = false
        return
    end
    local c = LP.Character
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h or h.Health <= 0 then
        Rival.forced = false; lowSamples = 0; highSamples = 0
    else
        local speed = h.WalkSpeed
        if speed <= 25 then
            lowSamples = math.min(lowSamples + 1, 2); highSamples = 0
            if lowSamples >= 2 then Rival.forced = true end
        elseif speed > 27 then
            highSamples = math.min(highSamples + 1, 2); lowSamples = 0
            if highSamples >= 2 then Rival.forced = false end
        end
    end
    rivalApplyEnabled()
end

task.spawn(function()
    while Rival.alive do
        rivalCheckSpeed()
        task.wait(0.18)
    end
end)

table.insert(Rival.connections, LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    rivalStop(); Rival.forced = false
    task.delay(0.35, function() if Rival.alive then rivalApplyEnabled() end end)
end))

-- Keybind del Rival
local rivalBound = resolveKey(cfg.rivalBind)
local rivalCapture = false

local function makeRowLabel(parent, text, y)
    local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    l.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 25)
    l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, y)
    l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    l.Text = text
    l.TextColor3 = COLORS.whiteChrome
    l.TextSize = 14
    l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    l.Parent = parent
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    st.Color = COLORS.greenNeon
    st.Thickness = 1
    st.Transparency = 0.3
    st.Parent = l
    return l
end

    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    c.Parent = obj
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    st.Color = COLORS.greenNeon
    st.Thickness = 1.5
    st.Parent = obj
end

    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Name = "Theme_" .. theme.name
    b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 22)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = theme.swatch
    b.Text = ""
    b.AutoButtonColor = true
    b.LayoutOrder = i
    b:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) -- el color del botón no se recolorea con el tema
    b.Parent = themeRow
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    c.Parent = b
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    st.Thickness = 2
    st.Transparency = 1
    st:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
    st.Parent = b
    themeBtns[i] = {btn = b, stroke = st}
end

local function recolorInstance(inst, map)
    local function remap(c)
        for _, pair in ipairs(map) do
            local o = pair[1]
            if math.abs(c.R - o.R) + math.abs(c.G - o.G) + math.abs(c.B - o.B) < 0.004 then
                return pair[2]
            end
        end
        return c
    end
    if inst:IsA("GuiObject") then inst.BackgroundColor3 = remap(inst.BackgroundColor3) end
    if inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton") or inst:IsA("TextBox") then
        inst.TextColor3 = remap(inst.TextColor3)
    end
    if inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then inst.ScrollBarImageColor3 = remap(inst.ScrollBarImageColor3) end
    if inst:IsA("UIStroke") then inst.Color = remap(inst.Color) end
    if inst:IsA("UIGradient") then
        local kps = {}
        for _, kp in ipairs(inst.Color.Keypoints) do
            table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, remap(kp.Value)))
        end
        inst.Color = ColorSequence.new(kps)
    end
end

local function applyThemeLive(idx)
    local map = applyPalette(idx)
    for _, inst in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
        if not inst:GetAttribute("NoTheme") then
            pcall(recolorInstance, inst, map)
        end
    end
    bgImage.ImageColor3 = themeTint(THEMES[idx])
end

local function refreshThemeUI()
    for i, data in ipairs(themeBtns) do
        data.stroke.Transparency = (cfg.themePick == i) and 0 or 1
    end
end

local bgPreviews = {}

local function refreshBackground()
    local style = cfg.bgPick
    if style == 0 or not BackgroundImages[style] then
        bgImage.Visible = false
        bgImage.Image = ""
    else
        bgImage.Visible = true
        loadImageInto(bgImage, BackgroundImages[style])
    end
    for idx, data in pairs(bgPreviews) do
        local selected = (idx == style)
        data.stroke.Color = selected and COLORS.greenNeon or COLORS.whiteEdge
        data.stroke.Thickness = selected and 3 or 1
        data.badge.Visible = selected
    end
end

local function makeBgPreview(order, styleIndex, imageId, displayName)
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = "Preview_" .. styleIndex
    btn.LayoutOrder = order
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.whiteChrome
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = true
    btn.Parent = gridHolder

    local btnCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    btnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    btnCorner.Parent = btn

    local btnStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    btnStroke.Color = COLORS.whiteEdge
    btnStroke.Thickness = 1
    btnStroke.Parent = btn

    if imageId then
        local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        img.Name = "Thumb"
        img.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -24)
        img.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
        img.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.greenBurn
        img.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        img.ImageTransparency = 0
        img.ZIndex = 2
        img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        img.Parent = btn
        loadImageInto(img, imageId)
        local imgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        imgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        imgCorner.Parent = img
    else
        local noneBox = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        noneBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -24)
        noneBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
        noneBox.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.greenBurn
        noneBox.Text = "✕"
        noneBox.TextColor3 = COLORS.whiteChrome
        noneBox.TextSize = 26
        noneBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        noneBox.Parent = btn
        local noneCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        noneCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        noneCorner.Parent = noneBox
    end

    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
    nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -19)
    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nameLabel.Text = displayName
    nameLabel.TextColor3 = COLORS.greenDeep
    nameLabel.TextSize = 11
    nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    nameLabel.Parent = btn

    local badge = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    badge.Name = "CheckBadge"
    badge.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
    badge.Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 6)
    badge.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
    badge.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.greenNeon
    badge.Text = "✓"
    badge.TextColor3 = COLORS.whiteChrome
    badge.TextSize = 11
    badge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    badge.Visible = false
    badge.ZIndex = 3
    badge.Parent = btn
    local badgeCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    badgeCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    badgeCorner.Parent = badge

    bgPreviews[styleIndex] = {btn = btn, stroke = btnStroke, badge = badge}

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        cfg.bgPick = styleIndex
        refreshBackground()
        saveConfig()
    end)
end

-------------------------------------------------
-- SMOOTH DRAGGING
-------------------------------------------------
local function makeDraggable(guiObject, dragHandle)
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil

    dragHandle.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos = guiObject.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                    local pos = guiObject.Position
                    cfg.guiPosXScale  = pos.X.Scale
                    cfg.guiPosXOffset = pos.X.Offset
                    cfg.guiPosYScale  = pos.Y.Scale
                    cfg.guiPosYOffset = pos.Y.Offset
                    saveConfig()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local delta = input.Position - dragStart
            guiObject.Position = UDim2.new(
                startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
end

makeDraggable(mainFrame, dragArea)

local function refreshUI()
    rivalBindBtn.Text = rivalCapture and "..." or (rivalBound and rivalBound.Name or "NONE")
end

local function toggleWindow()
    cfg.windowOpen = not cfg.windowOpen
    saveConfig()

    local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)

    if cfg.windowOpen then
        TweenService:Create(mainFrame, tweenInfo, {Size = EXPANDED_SIZE}):Play()
        task.wait(0.05)
        tabFrame.Visible = true
        collapsedContent.Visible = false
        if rivalTabBtn.BackgroundColor3 == COLORS.greenNeon then
            rivalContent.Visible = true
            configContent.Visible = false
        else
            rivalContent.Visible = false
            configContent.Visible = true
        end
        closeBtn.Text = "-"
    else
        rivalContent.Visible = false
        configContent.Visible = false
        tabFrame.Visible = false
        collapsedContent.Visible = true
        TweenService:Create(mainFrame, tweenInfo, {Size = COLLAPSED_SIZE}):Play()
        closeBtn.Text = "+"
    end
end

local function setActiveTab(active)
    for _, tab in ipairs({rivalTabBtn, configTabBtn}) do
        local isActive = (tab == active)
        tab.BackgroundColor3 = isActive and COLORS.greenNeon or COLORS.whiteChrome
        tab.TextColor3 = isActive and COLORS.whiteChrome or COLORS.greenDeep
    end
    rivalTabStroke.Color = (rivalTabBtn == active) and COLORS.greenGlow or COLORS.whiteEdge
    configTabStroke.Color = (configTabBtn == active) and COLORS.greenGlow or COLORS.whiteEdge

    rivalContent.Visible = (rivalTabBtn == active)
    configContent.Visible = (configTabBtn == active)
end

rivalTabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActiveTab(rivalTabBtn) end)
configTabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setActiveTab(configTabBtn) end)

local function updateScale()
    uiScale.Scale = guiScale
    scaleValueLabel.Text = tostring(math.floor(guiScale * 100)) .. "%"
    cfg.guiScale = guiScale
    saveConfig()
end

minusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if guiScale > 0.5 then
        guiScale = math.max(0.5, guiScale - 0.1)
        updateScale()
    end
end)

plusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if guiScale < 2.0 then
        guiScale = math.min(2.0, guiScale + 0.1)
        updateScale()
    end
end)

minimizeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleWindow)
closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleWindow)

-- RIVAL: UI
-------------------------------------------------
local function refreshRivalUI()
    local on = Rival.enabled
    rivalToggleBtn.Text = on and "DESACTIVAR CONFUSER" or "ACTIVAR CONFUSER"
    rivalToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = on and COLORS.greenNeon or COLORS.whiteChrome
    rivalToggleBtn.TextColor3 = on and COLORS.whiteChrome or COLORS.greenDeep
    rivalToggleStroke.Color = on and COLORS.greenGlow or COLORS.greenNeon

    collapsedToggleBtn.Text = on and "DESACTIVAR CONFUSER" or "ACTIVAR CONFUSER"
    collapsedToggleBtn.BackgroundColor3 = on and COLORS.greenNeon or COLORS.whiteChrome
    collapsedToggleBtn.TextColor3 = on and COLORS.whiteChrome or COLORS.greenDeep
    collapsedToggleStroke.Color = on and COLORS.greenGlow or COLORS.greenNeon

    if cfg.rivalAutoSteal then
        rivalStatusLbl.Text = Rival.forced and "STEAL SLOWDOWN DETECTED" or "WAITING FOR SLOWDOWN"
        rivalStatusLbl.TextColor3 = Rival.forced and COLORS.greenBright or COLORS.whiteMain
    else
        rivalStatusLbl.Text = "MANUAL CONTROL"
        rivalStatusLbl.TextColor3 = COLORS.whiteDim
    end

    rivalAutoBtn.Text = cfg.rivalAutoSteal and "ON" or "OFF"
    rivalAutoBtn.BackgroundColor3 = cfg.rivalAutoSteal and COLORS.greenNeon or COLORS.whiteChrome
    rivalAutoBtn.TextColor3 = cfg.rivalAutoSteal and COLORS.whiteChrome or COLORS.greenDeep

    for k, data in pairs(modeBtns) do
        local active = (Rival.mode == k)
        data.btn.BackgroundColor3 = active and COLORS.greenNeon or COLORS.whiteChrome
        data.btn.TextColor3 = active and COLORS.whiteChrome or COLORS.greenDeep
        data.stroke.Color = active and COLORS.greenGlow or COLORS.whiteEdge
    end

    rivalBindBtn.Text = rivalCapture and "..." or (rivalBound and rivalBound.Name or "NONE")
end

local function toggleRival()
    if cfg.rivalAutoSteal then cfg.rivalAutoSteal = false end
    Rival.manual = not Rival.enabled
    cfg.rivalManual = Rival.manual
    rivalApplyEnabled()
    refreshRivalUI()
    saveConfig()
end

rivalToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleRival)
collapsedToggleBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleRival)

rivalAutoBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    cfg.rivalAutoSteal = not cfg.rivalAutoSteal
    rivalApplyEnabled()
    refreshRivalUI()
    saveConfig()
end)

for k, data in pairs(modeBtns) do
    data.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if Rival.mode ~= k then
            Rival.mode = k
            cfg.rivalMode = k
            rivalStopTracks()
        end
        refreshRivalUI()
        saveConfig()
    end)
end

rivalBindBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    rivalCapture = true
    refreshRivalUI()
end)

rivalClearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    rivalCapture = false
    rivalBound = nil
    cfg.rivalBind = "NONE"
    refreshRivalUI()
    saveConfig()
end)

for i, data in ipairs(themeBtns) do
    data.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if cfg.themePick ~= i then
            cfg.themePick = i
            applyThemeLive(i)
            refreshRivalUI()
            saveConfig()
        end
        refreshThemeUI()
    end)
end
refreshThemeUI()

-------------------------------------------------
-- INPUT GLOBAL (keyboard + gamepad)
-------------------------------------------------
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    local kc = input.KeyCode
    local isGamepad = input.UserInputType.Name:match("^Gamepad") ~= nil

    if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and not isGamepad then return end

    -- Captura keybind Rival
    if rivalCapture then
        if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
            rivalCapture = false
            refreshRivalUI()
            return
        end
        if kc ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or
            (isGamepad and kc.Name:match("^Button"))) then
            rivalBound = kc
            cfg.rivalBind = kc.Name
            rivalCapture = false
            refreshRivalUI()
            saveConfig()
        end
        return
    end

    -- Toggle Rival (gamepad puede venir processed)
    if rivalBound and kc == rivalBound and (not processed or isGamepad) then
        toggleRival()
    end
end)

-- INIT

refreshUI()
refreshRivalUI()
AntiDie.Start()
rivalApplyEnabled()

    Rival.connections = {}
    Rival.alive = false
    if screenGui then pcall(function() screenGui:Destroy() end) end
end

            {title = "tp the 0", url = "https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/King%20Von%20-%20Took%20Her%20To%20The%20O%20(Lyrics)(MP3_160K).mp3", file = "RivalHub_overseer_to_the_o_filegarden.mp3"},
            {title = "laja", url = "https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/LAJA%20-%20NADIE%20TA%20FRIO%20(Letra)(MP3_160K).mp3", file = "RivalHub_overseer_laja_nadie_ta_frio_filegarden.mp3"},
            {title = "lucid", url = "https://file.garden/algLafWA1jk8WMfK/Lucid%20Dreams%20-%20Clean%20-%20Juice%20WRLD(MP3_160K).mp3", file = "RivalHub_overseer_lucid_dreams_filegarden.mp3"},
        }
        if M.index < 1 or M.index > #M.songs then M.index = 1 end

        pcall(function()
            local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            local old = cg:FindFirstChild("RivalHubMusicSound")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end)

        local assetFn = getcustomasset or getsynasset
        local currentSound, isDownloading, playToken = nil, false, 0

        local function validAudio(data)
            if type(data) ~= "string" or #data < 2048 then return false end
            local h = data:sub(1, 256):lower()
            return not (h:find("<html", 1, true) or h:find("<!doctype", 1, true) or h:find("access denied", 1, true) or h:find("not found", 1, true))
        end

        local function fileOk(path)
            if type(isfile) ~= "function" then return false end
            local ok, r = pcall(isfile, path)
            return ok and r == true
        end

        local function downloadSong(url, path)
            if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
            local data
            local reqFn = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
            if type(reqFn) == "function" then
                local ok, res = pcall(reqFn, {Url = url, Method = "GET", Headers = {["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0"}})
                if ok and type(res) == "table" then
                    local code = tonumber(res.StatusCode or res.Status or res.status_code) or 0
                    local body = res.Body or res.body
                    if (code == 0 or (code >= 200 and code < 300)) and validAudio(body) then data = body end
                end
            end
            if not data then
                local ok, r = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url, true) end)
                if ok and validAudio(r) then data = r end
            end
            if not data then return false end
            return (pcall(writefile, path, data))
        end

        local function stopSong()
            playToken = playToken + 1
            if currentSound then
                pcall(function() currentSound:Stop() end)
                pcall(function() currentSound:Destroy() end)
                currentSound = nil
            end
        end

        local function playSong()
            stopSong()
            if not M.enabled then return end
            local song = M.songs[M.index]
            if not song or type(assetFn) ~= "function" then return end
            local token = playToken
            task.spawn(function()
                if not fileOk(song.file) then
                    if isDownloading then return end
                    isDownloading = true
                    local ok = downloadSong(song.url, song.file)
                    isDownloading = false
                    if not ok then warn("[Rival Hub] No se pudo descargar: " .. song.title) return end
                end
                if token ~= playToken or not M.enabled then return end
                local ok, id = pcall(assetFn, song.file)
                if not ok or type(id) ~= "string" or id == "" then return end
                local s = Instance.new("Sound")
                s.Name = "RivalHubMusicSound"
                s.SoundId = id
                s.Volume = 0.75
                s.Looped = true
                if not pcall(function() s.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end) or not s.Parent then
                    s.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
                end
                currentSound = s
                pcall(function() s:Play() end)
            end)
        end

        local setMusicVisual = select(1, mkToggle(musicPage, "Music", function(on)
            M.enabled = on
            playSong()
        end))

        local songLabel
        do
            local row = mkRow(musicPage, 42)
            mkLabel(row, "Song")
            songLabel = mkSelector(row, M.songs[M.index].title, {}, function(dir, update)
                local n = #M.songs
                M.index = ((M.index - 1 + dir) % n) + 1
                update(M.songs[M.index].title)
                if M.enabled then playSong() end
            end)
            songLabel.TextScaled = true
            local c = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", songLabel)
            c.MaxTextSize = 12
            c.MinTextSize = 7
        end

        -- usado por loadAllSettings para restaurar estado guardado
        M.refresh = function()
            if M.index < 1 or M.index > #M.songs then M.index = 1 end
            songLabel.Text = M.songs[M.index].title
            if setMusicVisual then setMusicVisual(M.enabled) end
            playSong()
        end
    end

            local pos = 0

                if v == VX7A.index then pos = i end

            pos = pos + direction
            if pos < 0 then pos = #VX7A.best end
            if pos > #VX7A.best then pos = 0 end
            if pos == 0 then
                VX7A.stop()
            else
                VX7A.apply(VX7A.best[pos])
            end
            task.defer(function() pcall(saveAllSettings) end)
        end

    local xrayOn, brXrayOn = false, false
    local setXRayVisual, setBrXRayVisual
    setXRayVisual = mkToggle(visualPage, "X-Ray", function(on)
        xrayOn = on
        _G._RivalXrayOn = on
        pcall(setXRay, on)
    end)
    _G._RivalSetXRayVisual = setXRayVisual
    setBrXRayVisual = mkToggle(visualPage, "Brainrot X-Ray", function(on)
        brXrayOn = on
        _G._RivalBrXrayOn = on
        if _G.AmbitiousSetBrainrotXRay then pcall(_G.AmbitiousSetBrainrotXRay, on) end
    end)
    _G._RivalSetBrXRayVisual = setBrXRayVisual
    do
        -- Stretch Rez: solo el boton ON/OFF + flecha; al activarlo sale abajo la barrita para elegir cuanto
        local PANEL_H = 46
        local panelOpen, panelTween = false, nil
        local dd, arrow, arrowStroke

        local function setPanel(open)
            if open == panelOpen or not dd then return end
            panelOpen = open
            if panelTween then panelTween:Cancel() end
            if open then
                dd.Visible = true
                arrow.Text = "▲"
                arrowStroke.Transparency = 0
                panelTween = TS:Create(dd, TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, PANEL_H) })
                panelTween:Play()
            else
                arrow.Text = "▼"
                arrowStroke.Transparency = 0.25
                panelTween = TS:Create(dd, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), { Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0) })
                panelTween.Completed:Connect(function() if not panelOpen then dd.Visible = false end end)
                panelTween:Play()
            end
        end

        local function applyStretchState(s)
            stretchEnabled = s
            if s then enableStretch() else disableStretch() end
            setPanel(s)
        end

        local sv, row = mkToggle(visualPage, "Stretch Rez", function(on) applyStretchState(on) end)
        _G.stretchToggleSetter = function(s)
            sv(s)
            applyStretchState(s)
        end

        arrow = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        arrow.Name = "SelectorArrow"
        arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 26)
        arrow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0.5, 0)
        arrow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        arrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        arrow.Text = "▼"
        arrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        arrow.TextSize = 11
        arrow.AutoButtonColor = false
        arrow.ZIndex = 14
        Instance.new("UICorner", arrow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        arrowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", arrow)
        arrowStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        arrowStroke.Transparency = 0.25
        local function paintArrow()
            local col = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
            if rivalGuiColored and selectedColor then col = selectedColor end
            arrow.TextColor3 = col
            arrowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        end
        paintArrow()
        _rgSelRepaint[#_rgSelRepaint + 1] = paintArrow

        dd = Instance.new("Frame", visualPage)
        dd.Name = "StretchOptions"
        dd.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 0)
        dd.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        dd.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        dd.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dd.LayoutOrder = getNextOrder(visualPage)
        dd.Visible = false
        dd.ClipsDescendants = true
        dd.ZIndex = 7
        Instance.new("UICorner", dd).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11)

        arrow.Activated:Connect(function() setPanel(not panelOpen) end)

        -- ===== Barra con bolita (1 - 120) =====
        local fovLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", dd)
        fovLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 18)
        fovLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 5)
        fovLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        fovLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 207, 218)
        fovLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
        fovLbl.TextSize = 11
        fovLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        fovLbl.ZIndex = 8

        local track = Instance.new("Frame", dd)
        track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 4)
        track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 31)
        track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 47, 58)
        track.BorderSizePixel = 0
        track.ZIndex = 8
        Instance.new("UICorner", track).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local fill = Instance.new("Frame", track)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = getThemeColor()
        fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.ZIndex = 9
        Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local knob = Instance.new("Frame", track)
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.ZIndex = 10
        Instance.new("UICorner", knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local knobStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", knob)
        knobStroke.Color = getThemeColor(); knobStroke.Thickness = 1.5

        local function paintFov(v)
            local f = math.clamp(v / 120, 0, 1)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(f, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(f, 0, 0.5, 0)
            fovLbl.Text = "Stretch: " .. tostring(v)
        end
        -- v = 1..120 ; user = true cuando lo mueves tu (se guarda)
        -- El valor lo aplica enableStretch() (stretchFovConn) mientras Stretch Rez este activo
        _G.rivalSetFov = function(v, user)
            v = math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(v) or 120) + 0.5), 1, 120)
            stretchFOV = v
            _G._RivalFovSet = true
            paintFov(v)
            if user ~= false then task.defer(function() pcall(saveAllSettings) end) end
        end
        paintFov(math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(stretchFOV) or 120), 1, 120))

        local hit = Instance.new("TextButton", dd)
        hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 26)
        hit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 20)
        hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hit.Text = ""
        hit.AutoButtonColor = false
        hit.ZIndex = 11
        local sliding = false
        local function fromX(x)
            local rel = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
            _G.rivalSetFov(rel * 120)
        end
        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
            if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                sliding = true
                fromX(inp.Position.X)
                inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then sliding = false end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
            if sliding and (inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                fromX(inp.Position.X)
            end
        end)
